No file or raw YAML reader, Lua, secret resolution or store.

### ELZ-166

ELZ-166 — What follows the outcome of a graph node

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-166-node-outcome.md](../../tasks/ELZ-166-node-outcome.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** Pure rule for one settled graph node: the outcome routes of a node from the links of ELZ-161, and a total decision over the nine outcome classes of pattern-step: follow the route of that class, proceed after completed with no route, or fail the Run explicitly. No fallback between classes and no retry member, also for unknown. The decision is a Cure function with three named laws. No graph walk, join, map or loop rule: those belong to workflow/graph-run.

### ELZ-167

ELZ-167 — Check a recipe package before it can run

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-167-recipe-check.md](../../tasks/ELZ-167-recipe-check.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** Pure load-time check of a recipe package against facts that the caller supplies: a structural fault of ELZ-164 or ELZ-161 is refused with its reason atom at its part; then every tool resolves in the registry snapshot, each requirement of a tool is declared, each declared need is provided and each declared permission is granted, each cost is stated, and the exact upper cost of the root (sums, with map and loop maxima as factors) is at most the cap. Reasons are located path and code pairs in a fixed order; the same input gives the same verdict. It grants nothing and reserves nothing. The run-time portion follows as workflow/runtime-graph.

### ELZ-168

ELZ-168 — Graph pattern as one pure pattern step

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-168-graph-run.md](../../tasks/ELZ-168-graph-run.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** One graph callable of a recipe as a pure pattern step: ready nodes from needs, gives and links; a decision branch with a join that does not wait for the skipped branch; a map with a maximum count, a concurrency bound and a part-failure rule; a bounded loop; each outcome follows its link through workflow/node-outcome or the Run fails explicitly; a settled node never runs again and no outcome starts a second try; results in any arrival order give the same actions and finish; a nested callable gets only its typed input. Tested on recorded traces with one Cure law on the node state.

### ELZ-169

ELZ-169 — Accept a graph that a model gives at run time

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-169-runtime-graph.md](../../tasks/ELZ-169-runtime-graph.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** Pure acceptance of a graph that a model gives as plain data: it is added to the recipe of the Run as one more graph callable and checked by check_graph of workflow/recipe-check, with the limits of the agent: only its allowed callables and tool sources, no need or permission that the recipe does not declare, an upper cost within its budget share, a node maximum and a depth maximum, data only. A refused graph gives located reasons, rendered as content for the model with no value of the proposal. The same input gives the same verdict.

### ELZ-172

ELZ-172 — The run engine gives the result of a nested callable to its parent

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-172-engine-nested-result.md](../../tasks/ELZ-172-engine-nested-result.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** assembly/run-engine, changed in place by owner decision (no release yet, same Piece ID): the finish of a nested callable is kept in the durable record of the child, and the parent receives it as the completed outcome of its nested_callable action when its schema equals the declared result of the binding; another schema gives failed with nested_result_invalid; a child with no finish keeps nested_completed; the outcome survives a restart and the first finish counts. The root finish rule, the inputs of a pattern and the limits are unchanged.

### ELZ-170

ELZ-170 — The recipe version and source set that a Run keeps

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-170-version-pin.md](../../tasks/ELZ-170-version-pin.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** Pure rule of which recipe version and tool sources a Run uses: the version at its start, changed only by one explicit input; a publish and a change of settings change nothing in a running Run; the source set captured at the start never changes, a listing of an outside source is refused and no input adds a source. The step is a Cure function with three named laws.

### ELZ-171

ELZ-171 — Recipe versions of a tenant, with publish, pin and delete

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-171-recipe-store.md](../../tasks/ELZ-171-recipe-store.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** Immutable recipe versions of each tenant in state/artifact with access expiry and retention never; verdicts of workflow/recipe-check, the published pointer, pin records and delete marks in one stream per tenant under head-revision CAS. Only a checked version is published; go-back publishes an older version; two publishes and a pin with a delete each have one order; a pinned or published version is not deleted; the delete mark is committed before the Artifact delete; reap removes no recipe; a tenant cannot read another tenant.

Helpers are C functions of the one host, charge steps for their work, keep every limit as a sticky stop and read no clock. The planned ID runtime/lua-stdlib is removed with the index commit. This completes RC-60.

No callback into the caller and no state between runs. First portion of RC-62.

No call, no Lua, no discovery.

Tested on recorded traces.

One tool-registry source of kind pinned_skill with a load tool, whose answer is the declaration that workflow/agent-loop accepts, and a read-file tool, whose answer is marked content; the SKILL.md body is never marked or cut and scripts are bytes that are read, never run. A load lists no tool (coordinator decision).

### ELZ-245

ELZ-245 — Compare transactional command-store fits

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-245-command-store-fit.md](../../tasks/ELZ-245-command-store-fit.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** One bounded disposable-PostgreSQL spike runs through MIX_ENV=test mix run spikes/command-store-fit/run.exs under root Mix against actual Store/Postgres sources. The fixed fixture set is bounded to two trusted tenants, two actors and two operation IDs, with two resources of each caller type per tenant. A uses one shared real Store stream per tenant across both callers/resources, keeps per-resource revisions in folded state and fences fold-and-append with the current stream revision. B uses one receipt uniqueness key of (tenant, actor, operation_id) across caller types/resources. Both exclude target kind/resource ID from identity but include them in the canonical request digest; test map-order equivalence, equal repeats and same-identity conflicting bytes across distinct resources and caller types with valid independent CAS. Include authorization, rollback, concurrency, uncertainty, retention and outbox limits. Split-commit controls expose orphan participants; A replay bypass fails recorded-result and cross-target/type conflict assertions; removing B's identity fence fails those conflicts despite CAS. Prefer A only if all required cases pass for both callers; otherwise recommend B only if all B cases pass. If neither passes all cases, including complementary partial fits, report inconclusive and make no production recommendation. Report single-stream contention/replay limits. No production API is implemented.

### ELZ-246

ELZ-246 — Add a pure trailing-edge debounce policy

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-246-debounce.md](../../tasks/ELZ-246-debounce.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** A pure, bounded trailing-edge latest-binary-value policy accepts nonnegative-origin host time and bounded epoch/generation tokens, records accepted put and timeout samples including ignored well-formed tokens, preserves last time and generation on clear, reaches and uses maximum generation, and leaves state unchanged on rejection. Accepted empty-state and post-emission duplicate well-formed timeouts emit and schedule nothing while still recording their time sample; named Cure laws are distinguished from finite test evidence.

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-247-trigger-binding.md](../../tasks/ELZ-247-trigger-binding.md).

Errors do not echo values and claim no confidentiality for caller maps. It returns no_match, one checked binding or ambiguity and never loads, authenticates, authorizes, stores or starts a Run. The copied source closure declares existing cure and elixir providers, with no new provider or pin changes.

### ELZ-248

ELZ-248 — Distinguish private Git cleanup from published history

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-248-git-history-and-attribution.md](../../tasks/ELZ-248-git-history-and-attribution.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** The Git/privacy policy prohibits force-push, direct commits to main and rewriting published or shared history, including commits supplied for review or used by another worker. The sole owner may amend or reorganize their own unpublished local commits before sharing without separate permission while preserving intended and uncommitted work and Git identity. Standard Git author, committer and co-author metadata plus platform authorship attribution are allowed. Authored content, examples, commit-message prose and issue/PR prose remain roles-only; private environment details and session links remain forbidden. Protection/refusal rules, code-delta review, exact-head/local/hosted gates and queue rules are unchanged.

Its Memory and disposable PostgreSQL tests, including fault cases, and concrete PostgreSQL binding example with declared SQL dependency/provider closure stay inside the Piece. Mandatory later root acceptance ELZ-252 depends on ELZ-249, ELZ-250 and ELZ-127 and must invoke actual public RunEngine and pass real Memory/PostgreSQL composition including native parity before durable conversation, native parity or Wave 4 completion claims. ### ELZ-357

ELZ-357 — delivery/retry-timeout takes a retry delay from a not-dispatched failure, with optional full jitter

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-357-retry-delay-from-failure.md](../../tasks/ELZ-357-retry-delay-from-failure.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** In-place delivery/retry-timeout (settled owner decision 2 of the Wave 9 plan): policy/1 gains jitter (:none default | :full; else invalid_jitter) and max_failure_delay_ms (0..4,294,967,295, default 3,600,000; else invalid_max_failure_delay), checked after the existing checks; the Cure RetryPolicy record gains both fields (erasure a 7-tuple; today's 5-tuple is invalid_policy, no in-repo caller builds one); classify/1, decide/3 and backoff/2 keep their bodies and results. New pure delay(policy, attempts_made, class, %{failure_delay_ms: nil | n, random: 0..2^32-1}) -> {:ok, ms} (errors invalid_policy, invalid_attempt, invalid_class, invalid_delay_input in that order): with b = backoff and j = random*(b+1) div 2^32 under :full (0 under :none), a retryable class gives b or j without a failure delay and max(min(fd, max_failure_delay_ms), b) (+ j under :full) with one; completed, permanent and dispatched give exactly b. The Runner calls delay/4 with no failure delay and a :rand value drawn in its process (timers unchanged under :none). Selected Cure laws: delay of completed, permanent and dispatched equals backoff; the :none and :full retryable equalities; range bounds are test evidence (the pinned Cure rejects integer-inequality laws). Golden backoff/decide table recorded on the base, failure-delay edges under and over b and the clamp, jitter at random 0, 2^31 and 2^32-1 plus a sweep, dispatched keeps backoff, input boundary and order, two rejected Cure mutations in the audit test, a jittered Runner whose not-dispatched retry is jittered and whose dispatched retry in idempotent mode keeps b; five negative controls (failure delay on dispatched, no clamp, jitter under :none, the Runner passing :retryable for a dispatched retry, the Runner keeping backoff/2). Unknown-outcome policy: local small gate and full review. Output none.

### ELZ-363

ELZ-363 — Elzoo's bots as MCP tools: a verified caller lists the bots it may run, and a call is one Run with a bounded wait

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-363-mcp-server.md](../../tasks/ELZ-363-mcp-server.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** New integration/mcp-server (Wave 9 Q4 A): a Plug the host forwards to, bearer token verified by integration/token-verify before any MCP handling (401 otherwise), then ExMCP.HttpPlug 1.8.1 with a handler; tools/list gives each host-listed bot that domain/authorization allows the principal (proposal on bot/<id> run), tools/call decides again, checks arguments with core/payload-schema, starts one Run through a host start Need and polls a host result Need up to call_timeout_ms (1..60,000); an open Run returns its Run ID and goes on; each call is a new Run; results bounded to 65,536 bytes, no token echoed. Coordinator decision 2026-11-24: tools/list also leaves out a bot whose start schema is not object-rooted (MCP inputSchema rule), and case 3 calls no Need beyond the trusted clock and the verifier's own. The ready mcp-client in loopback mode against the Plug on a loopback socket with scripted Run Needs; negative controls on the authorization decision, the schema check and the token-before-handler order. Untrusted input and auth: local small gate and full review.

### ELZ-364

ELZ-364 — integration/ai-text takes images in a user message by artifact reference, for a provider that declares image input

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-364-ai-text-image-input.md](../../tasks/ELZ-364-ai-text-image-input.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** In-place integration/ai-text Contract amendment 2 (Wave 9 Q7 A, absorbs integration/ai-multimodal): a user message may hold parts (text, and image by state/artifact reference with media type png/jpeg/webp/gif, at most 8 images); the capability set gains :image_input (undeclared is unsupported_feature); a host artifacts Need resolves each reference before translation at admission and in the Adapter, checking record identity, media type, size and the hash_format-prefixed SHA-256 and per-image/total bounds; a failure is the local refusal input_unavailable at admission and the new attested no-send tag input_unavailable at dispatch (Cure decision, table and no_send updated); document stays refused; a request without parts is byte for byte today's (golden recorded on the base first); a conformance scenario :image_input. Coordinator decision 2026-11-24: delivery/provider-stream prepare/2 and integration/ai-token-count estimate/1, which call Request.decode/1 without check/2, refuse a parts message as unsupported_feature (their README and piece.json follow; index rows updated). Negative controls on the capability check, the hash check, the no-send mark and each of the two parts refusals. Local small gate and full review. Output none.

### ELZ-365

ELZ-365 — One payment's charge and refunds through a provider Need, with unknown outcomes reconciled, verified events applied forward only, and the compensation of a succeeded charge

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-365-payment-lifecycle.md](../../tasks/ELZ-365-payment-lifecycle.md).

**Product aims:** P10.

**Acceptance:** New durable provider-neutral domain/payment-lifecycle (Wave 9 Q9 D, Q10 A; replaces RC-24 for this output; integration/payments waits): payments and refunds under the tenant floor, each provider call one delivery/effect-intent intent with logical reference <payment_id>/charge or <payment_id>/refund/<refund_id> (IDs without '/', except the reserved comp/charge) so the provider key never derives per Attempt; charge/3 checks the stored approval digest (approval_mismatch); two Adapters (charge, refund) store each provider answer as an observation (a failed write keeps the intent dispatched for a later poll), which record/3 applies (with a host fallback for an intent with no result), and record/3 and apply_event/3 apply only forward transitions; a verified event settles the row while the intent waits for poll/1; refund/3 under the payment row lock refuses over_refund; the succeeded charge returns its compensation {refund, comp/charge, :remaining}, returned whenever the charge is succeeded, which refunds what is still refundable when it runs (PR #853 answer). Coordinator decision 2026-11-24: the ChargeAdapter refuses a send of a final charge, or of one with a stored decline or completed final answer, as the attested no-send charge_closed, and a failed pre-send read in either Adapter is the attested no-send store_unavailable. Scripted provider; RC-24's cases, compensation twice and after a partial refund, elzoo_app isolation; negative controls on a per-Attempt key, the refund lock, forward-only events and the write-failure envelope. Local small gate and full review.

### ELZ-366

ELZ-366 — A tenant's subscription to a plan version: UTC monthly or yearly periods from an anchor, a trial, renewal with the period's fee line and grant, changes at the next period, cancel at period end or now

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-366-plan.md](../../tasks/ELZ-366-plan.md).

**Product aims:** P09, P10.

**Acceptance:** New durable domain/plan (Wave 9 Q11 A): one subscription per tenant to a plan version with a domain/money fee, UTC monthly or yearly periods from the anchor clamped to the month's last day (Cure rule; selected literal laws, every anchor day as an exhaustive test against an independent oracle), trial, renew/3 advancing exactly one period when due (optionally renew_ahead_ms before the boundary) under the row lock and returning the period's fee line and its domain/entitlement grant <subscription_id>/<index> (non-overlapping), repeats returning equal values; change at the next renewal, no proration; cancel at period end or now with grant revocations (inside the renew-ahead window at the running period's end, with the next period's grant revoked and its fee line withdrawn); past-due and paid marks; expected revisions. Negative controls on the clamp, anchor chaining, the revision check and the ahead-window cancel. Local small gate and full review.

### ELZ-367

ELZ-367 — Numbered invoices of one currency: usage, fee and adjustment lines, gapless numbers per series, payment application and reversal, void and credit notes

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-367-invoice.md](../../tasks/ELZ-367-invoice.md).

**Product aims:** P09, P10.

**Acceptance:** New durable domain/invoice (Wave 9 Q11 A): one-currency invoices with usage lines from a complete domain/pricing charge (incomplete_charge otherwise), plan fee lines and adjustments; finalize/3 takes the next gapless number of the series under the series row lock and names the journal operation invoice/<id>/issue, mark_posted/3 records the host's result; apply_payment/3 up to the payable (total minus issued credit notes), idempotent by payment_ref; reverse_payment/3 takes an amount (or :remaining) of one applied payment up to what is still applied, idempotent by reversal_id (PR #853 answer); void with nothing paid or credited; credit notes within the payable, finalized issued with a negative total, reduce the corrected invoice's payable. Concurrent numbering, partial reversal after a partial refund, elzoo_app isolation; negative controls on numbering outside the lock, another currency and whole reversal. Local small gate and full review.

### ELZ-368

ELZ-368 — The merge queue waits for every required check on the exact head before it merges

**Acceptance:** Defect #887: before gh pr merge the queue reads the required status checks of main from its branch protection and waits until the newest check run of each one on the exact head has completed, including gleam-hosted when no Gleam path changed; a non-success conclusion stops without a merge, an unreadable list refuses, and the pre-merge checks follow the wait. Evidence, modes and runs-on unchanged. Guard tests with a negative control, a read-only live check, full local gate and full hosted check.

### ELZ-369

ELZ-369 — Pure model routing: the eligible candidates of a route in rank order, and the next step after each Attempt's outcome, with no fallback after an unknown outcome

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-369-model-routing.md](../../tasks/ELZ-369-model-routing.md).

**Product aims:** P09, P13.

**Acceptance:** New pure delivery/model-routing (Wave 9 Q6 A, Q8 A; replaces RC-25 for this output): new/1 checks a route of 1..16 candidate refs (provider, region, retention, model_id, ai-text features, unique rank 1..1,000), on_unknown :stop, max_health_age_ms, cool_down_ms and a retry-timeout policy without mode (known keys only, max_attempts at most 256); select/3 gives eligible refs in rank order and each excluded one with the first reason of tried, capability, {data_policy, deny} (domain/provider-data-policy decide/2), stale_health, circuit_open, cooling_down, budget, or no_candidate; after_failure/3 maps an ai-text outcome to done, {retry_same, ms} for a not-sent refusal or a local rate refusal (RetryTimeout.delay/4 with the failure delay, capped at max_failure_delay_ms), {next, cool_down} for throttled/overloaded (retry_after seconds or cool_down_ms), {next, nil} for authentication_failed/permission_denied/not_found, {next, nil} also for the admission refusal unsupported_feature (per provider), {stop, request_fault} for invalid_request/too_large and {stop, unknown_outcome} (no fallback after unknown). Coordinator checks: unsupported_feature is an admission refusal, retry_after is seconds, health facts are host-stamped; circuit-breaker, rate-limit and budget are host facts, so no dependency on ELZ-100/280/022; the PR #853 :tried answer carried. It decides what follows an unknown outcome: local small gate and full review.

### ELZ-378

ELZ-378 — A durable checkout that pays an invoice or tops up a wallet, records each step's compensation with its result, and undoes the completed steps in reverse order after a definite failure

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-378-ledger-checkout.md](../../tasks/ELZ-378-ledger-checkout.md).

**Product aims:** P10.

**Acceptance:** New assembly/ledger-checkout (Wave 9 Q9 D, Q10 A, settled decision 1; replaces RC-41 for this output): checkout and step tables under the tenant floor; an invoice payment (charge through payment-lifecycle create/charge/get with payment_id checkout.<id>, invoice apply_payment with payment_ref checkout/<id>, journal post checkout/<id>/settle) or a top-up (charge, wallet post, optional host allowance grant Need with a declared parallel group); each step recorded before it starts, its result and compensation in one checkout transaction, calls to other Pieces outside it; a pending or unknown charge waits with nothing registered; a definite failure compensates in reverse order (comp/charge refund of the rest, reverse_payment, journal reverse, revoke), each idempotent by its key; a failed compensation stops needs_operator; resume/3 holds a session advisory lock per checkout (a second caller calls nothing and waits busy) and after a crash re-asks by key; a parallel group starts both members and is decided when both are definite; in_transaction and isolation record nothing; list_running/3 bounded, for open or needs_operator checkouts. Coordinator decision (2026-11-24, automated review of PR #917): each charge pass calls create/3 first (conflict a definite failure) and an existing payment of another amount or recipient is approval_mismatch, never adopted. Coordinator checks: no quota allowance function on main, so the allowance is a host Need; plan not called (fee lines are invoice data). Durable money state: local small gate and full review.

code mode through a harness binding that builds the lua-tool spec (declared calls complete, an undeclared function sandbox_refused unlisted_name, steps, the bytecode signature lua_error, an expired module call reported under expiry_reports: :owner); doors - the mounted mcp-server reached by the ready client (per-principal lists, one Run per call, the running bound, invalid_arguments, an expired token 401, another tenant's valid token not refused but scoped, a legacy session 404, listen -32601), webhook-out signed deliveries verified by a local receiver (dedup 503 then 2xx with one webhook-id, non-dedup dead-lettered, a private address at send not sent then retries_exhausted); models - routing over ai-text-compatible and ai-text-google stubs (throttle with retry_after to a new Attempt with unknown usage metered, :tried then :cooling_down, a data-policy denial, own credentials only, an image on the start only to the image-capable candidate (coordinator decision 2026-11-24: a message reaches an agent-loop root under run-engine only with no active slot, #930), unknown stops, a local rate wait); money - a trial subscription renewing on 28 February, a numbered invoice posted once, a checkout with duplicate and late events, reverse-order compensation, needs_operator, over_refund, an unknown charge until poll, approval_mismatch, a no-negative wallet top-up with a scripted provider; owner-only DDL, tenant reads and 42501 inserts on every new table; update refused by privilege on the three insert-only tables; five harness negative controls (three replace plan controls that cannot be performed). Full gate (mix check --affected origin/main), full review, merge queue. Output none.

ELZ-387 — assembly/run-engine: an opt-in start option gives a message to the root while slots are active

Publication copy: [tasks/ELZ-387-run-engine-messages-while-active.md](../../tasks/ELZ-387-run-engine-messages-while-active.md).

**Product aims:** P13.

**Acceptance:** In-place assembly/run-engine for #930 (owner decision 25 Nov, option A), same Piece ID: optional start option messages: :while_active (only value; recorded in configure control and fingerprint; absent = today's engine byte for byte; added or removed on restart is incompatible_recovery); with it, a message is given to the root when the lifecycle permits new work or decides RemainWaiting, also while slots are active (unsettled or holding a pending input), as an input that settles no slot (no slot, join, Budget or wait change; :none charges nothing; a request charges one count and is admitted as usual); root only, message only (wake, start and resource_event unchanged); in its own fold step after its expiry pass, before the reports that pass holds (dropped if that step closes generation); paused, past the pause deadline, terminal, Run deadline, root Budget at its cap, late, duplicate, closed generation, pending stop, closed root and no invocation room stay refused; transcript adds only its :input entry (expiry settlements of its pass as for any input), status keeps its keys. agent-loop's 'No engine claim' sentence names the option; its step, Contract and piece.json unchanged. Tests: default off with the #930 shape, delivery while a tool, local or idle nested slot is open, v2 with a first excess that the readers fold, a request from a message and its first excess, root only, wake, start, resource_event and pause refused (re-send under a new ID), order with expiry reports (and their drop at a first excess), room and replay; eight negative controls. Durable state and unknown outcomes: local small gate and full review. Output none.
